Encoder 1311, world 1: decision 12

Gallery index

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

A_only is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged pipeline._report_text helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside <pre> blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

01. s1311_w01_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: baseline.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 09fb1494c512a9cc8ee98e69e200e4f85a485abf5b5e0ea4efcdfa1a0a4488d7.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 gives fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (view=current) peaks: n0 blue at [0,0] (peak probability ~0.9598), n0 red at [0,2] (~0.99999); n1 blue and red each tie among [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]; n2 blue and red tie among [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]; n3 blue at [1,3] (~0.9983), n3 red at [0,0] (~0.9990). Origins shown in the packet indicate some current distributions were copied or inherited from prior records; those copied prior records are not new estimates. Candidate conditional records (commands k0,k1,k2; view=candidate) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Conditional-record differences concentrate at node n1: under k0 the blue peak is [1,2] (~0.9595) and red peak [0,0] (~0.9723); under k1 blue [1,2] (~0.8668) and red [0,0] (~0.99924); under k2 blue [1,2] (~0.6255) and red [0,0] (~0.99957). Other candidate nodes (n0,n2,n3) keep the same peak cells across commands as in current. No physical presence, experiences or calibrated confidence are asserted.

02. s1311_w01_t12_A_A_only_current_donor

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: current_donor.

Narrator text SHA-256: 0642f18462369238bd4f81560fee9862cce1d0bfe769ec91a131a716611a0400.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Copied-prior origins in this packet are not new estimates, and conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are alternative, not already-executed, branches. Current (represented_time 12–13) peaks: n0 — blue peaks at (0,0) (peak ≈0.96), red at (0,2) (≈1.0). n1 — both roles are currently multimodal with four equal peak cells {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)} (blue peak ≈0.1383, red ≈0.2486). n2 — both roles peak on the four-cell set {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)} (blue ≈0.1517, red ≈0.2449). n3 — blue peaks at (1,3) (≈0.9054), red at (0,0) (≈0.9938). Conditional-command differences: nodes n0, n2 and n3 have the same peak cells under k0,k1,k2 as current; node n1 shifts under commands toward blue peaking at (1,2) (k0≈0.9595, k1≈0.8668, k2≈0.6255) and red peaking at (0,0) (k0≈0.9723, k1≈0.99924, k2≈0.99957). No physical facts beyond these distributional descriptions are asserted.

Narrator text SHA-256: 17bcb830863a2a70f93d5705762324600ce5b16d8a8d10a399734dbedb17a6f5.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 current estimates (inherited/copied origins are not new estimates): n0 — blue concentrated at cell (0,0) and red at (0,2) with very high peak probabilities. n1 current — both blue and red are multimodal with equal peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1). n2 — blue and red share four symmetric peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3) (different peak probabilities per role). n3 — blue concentrated at (1,3) and red at (0,0). Command-conditioned (candidate) estimates are hypothetical, not executed events: nodes n0, n2 and n3 have the same peak cells under each candidate as in current. Node n1 differs by command: under k0 blue peaks at (1,2) (high probability) and red at (0,0); under k1 blue still peaks at (1,2) but with lower peak probability and red at (0,0); under k2 blue peaks at (1,2) with yet lower peak probability and red at (0,0).

Narrator text:

This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for 'blue' and 'red' at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Origins in the record include copied prior records and inherited estimates; copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional branches, not executed events. Current views (view=current) summarize the present distributions per panel: in both panels n0’s blue distribution is peaked at cell (0,0) and red at (0,2); n1 in current p0 and p1 shows four equal local peaks across the 2x2 upper-left block; n2 in both panels concentrates on the four-cell block at columns 2–3, rows 0–1 for both roles; n3’s blue peaks at (1,3) and red peaks at (0,0) (panels differ in conditional branches). Conditional records show variation: for example at p0,n1 the blue peak is concentrated at (1,2) under k0/k1/k2 with differing peak probabilities across commands. Treat these statements as descriptions of the supplied distributions only.

Under conditional commands, node n1 often concentrates blue at [1,2] and red at [0,0] or (in p1,k2) becomes uniform; peak probabilities shift across k0–k2 (examples in propositions). Narrator text:

This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) of fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current entries (represented_time 12–13) are inherited/completed records; copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command candidate records (k0,k1,k2) are alternative conditional estimates, not events that have occurred. Current summaries: n0 — blue peaks at (0,0) and red at (0,2). n1 — both roles currently have four equal-mode peaks in the upper-left 2×2 block {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}. n2 — both roles peak over the right-side four cells {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}. n3 — blue peaks at (1,3) and red at (0,0). Conditional-command candidates change some node-n1 forecasts: in several candidate records blue concentrates at (1,2) with peak probability varying across k0→k1→k2, while other conditional records for n1 are more spread or uniform. The packet is presented as data only.

Narrator text:

This packet contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for blue and red roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current entries inherit prior records represented at time 12 (copied or completed records) and are not new estimates; candidate entries are conditional command estimates (k0,k1,k2) and do not represent executed events. In current p0 and p1, n0 blue peaks at cell (0,0) and red at (0,2); p0 and p1 n3 blue peaks at (1,3) and red at (0,0). Node n2 in both panels has four tied peak cells ([0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) for both roles. Node n1 shows differences: current peaks in p0 are the 2×2 block ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]); candidate commands change n1 peaks across panels and commands (examples: k0 often places a single peak at [1,2] in p0; k2 in p1 gives a uniform peak set over all 16 cells). Use the proposition list for the exact peak cells for each panel, node, role, view and command.

Narrator text:

This packet contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Row increases downward, column rightward. Origins in the data identify copied or completed prior records versus native conditional records; copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are conditional proposals, not events that have occurred. Current (non‑conditional) estimates: in both panels, n0’s blue peak is at cell [0,0] and red peak at [0,2]; n1 in current p0 and p1 has multi‑modal peaks on the 2×2 block {[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]} for both roles; n2’s current peaks are the four cells {[0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]} for both roles; n3’s current blue peak is [1,3] and red peak is [0,0]. Conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) change some node estimates: for example at panel p0 node n1 blue shifts peak between [1,2] (k0,k1) and [0,0] (k2) with differing peak probabilities; in panel p1 node n1 k2 is a uniform peak set (all 16 cells) unlike other candidate records that concentrate on small sets. Treat all quoted peaks and reported uncertainties as distributional summaries only.

26. s1311_w01_t12_ordinary_joined_prospective_restored

Narrator text SHA-256: 5875fe93314b4aa9572bd68eb5bdd811e0778bbf6a8f8b5fbbd9f2554c83cce8.

Narrator text:

This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) of fallible location distributions for red and blue roles at four addressed nodes (n0–n3) on separate 4×4 grids (rows down, columns right). Panel entries labeled current are inherited or completed records for represented times shown; candidate entries (commands k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. For panel p0 current: n0 peaks — blue at (0,0), red at (0,2); n1 peaks — blue and red across the 2×2 block (rows 0–1, cols 0–1); n2 peaks — both roles on the four cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); n3 peaks — blue at (1,3), red at (0,0). Panel p1 current: n0 peaks — blue (0,0), red (0,2); n1 peaks — 2×2 block (0–1,0–1) for both roles; n2 peaks — same four-cell set as p0; n3 peaks — blue (1,3), red (0,0). Candidate records show alternative peak sets per command as listed in propositions; these are conditional estimates, not claims that those commands occurred.

Narrator text:

Panels p0 and p1 each present fallible 4x4 location distributions for blue and red at nodes n0–n3. Candidate records (view=candidate, command=k0/k1/k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events; some entries are inherited/copied from prior records and are not new estimates. Conditional estimates differ by command at some nodes: for example node n1 under several candidate commands sometimes concentrates blue at (1,2) with high peak probability (k0/k1 in p0 and some k1 in p1), while other candidate records leave n1 multimodal or uniform (notably k2 in panel p1 is uniform). Treat all quoted probabilities and peaks as properties of the supplied distributions only.

Narrator text:

This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on a 4×4 grid in two panels (p0, p1). Current estimates (view=current) are identical across panels: n0 — blue peaks at (0,0), red at (0,2); n1 — both roles spread over the four-cell block {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}; n2 — both roles peak on the right two columns of the top two rows {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}; n3 — blue peaks at (1,3), red at (0,0). Candidate command records differ mainly at node n1: under k1 blue concentrates at (1,2) while red concentrates at (0,0); under k2 both roles are uniform across all 16 cells; under k0 n1 matches the current four-cell pattern. Nodes n0, n2 and n3 show the same peak cells across current and candidate records. Origins and times in the packet indicate which entries are copied or conditional; the candidate records are conditional estimates, not executed events.
